come  true.  1 am  also  indebted  to  my  fadier,  (Late)  Denis  Dkhar  and 
my  mother,  Sumer  Sisilia  Buam  for  igniting  in  me  the  desire  for 
knowledge  and  learning  at  an  early  age.

It  would  be  near  impossible  for  me  to  mention  the  names  of  all  those 
who  have  contributed  in  the  process  of  writing  this  book;  so,  I will 
mention  a few  at  the  risk  of  offending  others.

Being  a Var-)aintia  by  birth,  my  association  with  the  Pnar  people, 
with  whom  I'm  constandy  in  touch  with,  particularly,  my  wife,  Mrs, 
loptimon  Dkhar,  and  friends,  Mr.  Riquoma  Rq  Laloo,  Mr,  R.  Konel 
Phira,  Mr.  Sajeki  Passah  and  many  others  have  enriched  my  Pnar 
vocabulary  and  emboldened  me  to  write  this  novel  in  Pnar.

1 also  put  on  record  the  guidance  I received  from  Dr.  B.  Myrboh,  Dr. 
B.  Kharbuli,  Dr.  H.  Kayang,  Dr.  Don  Syiem  and  Dr.  B.  S.  Khongwir  and 
all  my  colleagues  at  North  Eastern  Biodiversity  Research  Cell,  NEHU. 
The  experience  I gained  there  helped  me  in  writing  this  book.

The  support  and  'ego  massage'  given  by  friends  and  relatives  has 
always  reaffirmed  my  faith  in  myself  and  goaded  me.  'Thank  you 
loannis  Thamar,  Samson  Pohthmi,  Blassius  Buam,  ].  Joseph  Buam,  K. 
S.  Lyngdoh,  Rimiki  Ksoo,  E Nangbah,  S.  Kharpuri  and  others.

I am  deeply  grateful  to  the  efforts  of  Mr.  S.  Q.  Sumer,  Mr.  Riquoma  Rq 
Laloo  and  Mr.  loannis  Thamar  for  proofreading  the  manuscripts  and 
enriching  the  novel  with  their  suggestions.  Any  flaws  in  this  book 
are  mine.

Thank  You  all.  Chihajarqdh.

wan  nai]  rkhai  Ya  ki  hadoh  wa  ksoh  kpoh.  U
